Event ID: 7497 (Derived from 6.2.1)
Sentence: "- Termination of RAN CP interface (N2)."
Start State: The RAN CP interface (N2) is established and active between a Radio Access Network (RAN) node and the 5G Core Network (5GC).
Condition: A trigger (e.g., UE deregistration, handover to non-3GPP access, RAN node failure, or network-initiated release) occurs that necessitates the removal of the N2 association.
Action: The procedure to terminate the N2 interface is executed, which involves releasing the NGAP association and related resources between the RAN and the AMF.
End State: The RAN CP interface (N2) is released and is no longer active for the specific UE context or RAN node association.
============================================================
Event ID: 7498 (Derived from 6.2.1)
Sentence: "- Termination of NAS (N1), NAS ciphering and integrity protection."
Start State: NAS (N1) connection exists with active ciphering and integrity protection.
Condition: A trigger (e.g., deregistration, connection release) requires termination of the NAS signaling connection and its security context.
Action: The network and UE terminate the NAS (N1) signaling connection and deactivate the associated NAS ciphering and integrity protection algorithms.
End State: NAS (N1) connection is released, and NAS ciphering and integrity protection are no longer active.

Event ID: 7610 (Derived from 6.2.3)
Sentence: "- Downlink packet buffering and downlink data notification triggering."
Start State: Not explicitly defined. Typically, the UE is in CM-IDLE or CM-CONNECTED with RRC Inactive state, and a downlink packet arrives at the UPF for the UE.
Condition: A downlink data packet arrives for a UE that is not in CM-CONNECTED with RRC Active state, requiring the network to page the UE and re-establish the user plane.
Action: The UPF buffers the downlink packet and sends a Downlink Data Notification (DDN) to the SMF, which then triggers network-initiated Service Request procedures (e.g., by notifying the AMF).
End State: The downlink packet is buffered, and the process to notify the control plane (SMF/AMF) to page the UE and establish a connection has been initiated.

Event ID: 7613 (Derived from 6.2.3)

Start State: Not explicitly defined. The GTP-U layer is operating without packet duplication or elimination features being actively applied for a specific PDU session or QoS flow.

Action: In the downlink direction, the transmitting GTP-U entity duplicates packets. In the uplink direction, the receiving GTP-U entity performs packet elimination, discarding redundant duplicate packets.
End State: Packet duplication is active in the downlink GTP-U path, and packet elimination is active in the uplink GTP-U path for the specified QoS flow, improving transmission reliability.

Sentence: "NOTE 3: In the case of SNPN and if the UE provides an IMSI type SUCI to the AMF and the SUCI provided by UE or the SUPI derived from the SUCI is for an SNPN served by the AMF, the AMF uses the selected NID provided by the NG-RAN together with the selected PLMN ID (from IMSI) or the Routing Indicator provided by the UE within the SUCI for selection of AUSF. Start State: The AMF has received a registration request from a UE in an SNPN, containing a SUCI.
Condition: The UE is in an SNPN and provides a SUCI to the AMF. Two sub-conditions exist: 1) The SUCI is of IMSI type and the SUCI/derived SUPI is for an SNPN served by the AMF. 2) The SUCI is of NSI type.

For the NSI-type SUCI case, the AMF uses the Home Network Identifier and Routing Indicator from the SUCI/SUPI to select an AUSF.
End State: The AMF has determined the parameters to use for AUSF selection.

Event ID: 8224 (Derived from 6.3.5)
Sentence: "- In the case of AMF discovery and selection functionality in AMF or other CP NFs use GUAMI (in the SNPN case, along with NID of the SNPN that owns the AMF instances to be discovered and selected) or TAI to discover the AMF instance(s), the NRF provides the NF profile of the associated AMF instance(s). If an associated AMF is unavailable due to AMF failure, the NF profile of the backup AMF used for failure is provided by the NRF. If no AMF instances related to the indicated GUAMI can be found, the NRF may provide a list of NF profiles of candidate AMF instances in the same AMF Set. If no NF profiles of AMF is returned in the discovery result, the other CP NF or AMF may discover an AMF using the AMF Set as below."
Start State: An AMF or other CP NF needs to discover and select an AMF instance.
Condition: AMF discovery and selection functionality is invoked using GUAMI (with NID for SNPN) or TAI.
Action: The NRF provides the NF profile(s) of the associated AMF instance(s). In cases of unavailability (planned removal or failure), the NRF provides the profile of the relevant backup AMF. If the GUAMI's AMF pointer maps to multiple AMFs, all are provided. If no AMF matches the GUAMI, the NRF may provide candidate AMF profiles from the same AMF Set, from which the requesting NF may select. If no profiles are returned, the requesting NF may proceed to discover an AMF using the AMF Set.
End State: The requesting NF has received NF profile(s) for AMF selection or has a path to discover an AMF via the AMF Set.

Condition: The UE is to perform non-3GPP access node selection.
Action: The UE shall attempt to determine its current country using implementation-specific methods. If the country cannot be determined, the UE shall stop the non-3GPP access node selection procedure.
End State: The UE has either determined its country and proceeds, or has stopped the non-3GPP access node selection.

End State: The UE is ready to execute the actions specified for the "home country" scenario.

Condition: The UE fails to establish a connection to an ePDG or N3IWF within the selected HPLMN.

Event ID: 8310 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "a) If the UE is registered via 3GPP access to a PLMN and this PLMN is included in the Non-3GPP access node selection information, then the UE shall select this PLMN. End State: The UE has selected a PLMN for Non-3GPP access, either the initial one or an alternative one via DNS.

Event ID: 8316 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "- First, the UE shall perform a DNS query using the Visited Country FQDN for N3IWF, as specified in TS 23.003 [19] to determine if the visited country mandates the selection of N3IWF in this country."
Start State: The UE is in a visited country and needs to establish a connection via a Non-3GPP InterWorking Function (N3IWF).
Condition: The UE is initiating a procedure to select an N3IWF in a visited country.
Action: The UE performs a DNS query using the Visited Country FQDN for N3IWF, as specified in TS 23.003 [19].
End State: The UE has obtained a DNS response indicating whether the visited country mandates the use of a local N3IWF.
============================================================
Event ID: 8317 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "The DNS response received by the UE may be empty or may contain the identities of one or more PLMNs in the visited country, which may be used for N3IWF selection, if the UE decides to select an N3IWF, as specified below. For example, the DNS response may contain the identity of PLMN-1 and the identity of PLMN-2."
Start State: The UE has sent a DNS query for N3IWF discovery.
Condition: The UE receives a DNS response to its N3IWF discovery query.
Action: The UE processes the DNS response, which may be empty or may contain one or more PLMN identities (e.g., PLMN-1, PLMN-2) from the visited country.
End State: The UE has received information (which may be empty) that can be used for N3IWF selection.
============================================================
Event ID: 8318 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "- Then, the UE shall perform a DNS query using the Visited Country FQDN for ePDG, as specified in TS 23.003 [19] to determine if the visited country mandates the selection of ePDG in this country. The DNS response received by the UE may be empty or may contain the identities of one or more PLMNs in the visited country, which may be used for ePDG selection, if the UE decides to select an ePDG, as specified below. For example, the DNS response may contain the identity of PLMN-1 and the identity of PLMN-3."
Start State: The UE is in a visited country and needs to determine the local policy for ePDG selection.
Condition: The UE is following the procedure to discover if the visited country mandates ePDG selection.
Action: The UE performs a DNS query using the Visited Country FQDN for ePDG as per TS 23.003 [19] and receives a DNS response that may be empty or contain one or more PLMN identities.
End State: The UE has received a DNS response indicating the visited country's ePDG selection policy and available PLMNs for potential ePDG selection.
============================================================

Sentence: "- If the UE does not receive a DNS response in none of the above two DNS queries, then the UE shall stop the non-3GPP access node selection. Otherwise, the next steps are executed."

Condition: The UE does not receive a DNS response for either of the two DNS queries.
Action: The UE shall stop the non-3GPP access node selection procedure.
End State: The non-3GPP access node selection procedure is terminated.

Condition: The UE has received the two DNS responses as part of the initial registration procedure for non-3GPP access.
Action: The UE consolidates the PLMN identities from the two DNS responses and constructs a candidate list of PLMNs.
End State: A candidate list of PLMNs is constructed by the UE.

Event ID: 8326 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "- If the UE is registered via 3GPP access to a PLMN which is included in the candidate list of PLMNs, then the UE shall select this PLMN. If the UE fails to connect to a non-3GPP access node in this PLMN, then the UE shall select a different PLMN included in the candidate list of PLMNs as specified in the next bullet."

Event ID: 8327 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "- If the UE is registered via 3GPP access to a PLMN which is not included in the candidate list of PLMNs, or the UE is not registered via 3GPP access to any PLMN, or the UE fails to connect to a non-3GPP access node according to the previous bullet, then the UE shall select one of the PLMNs included in the candidate list of PLMNs based on the prioritized list of PLMNs in the Non-3GPP access node selection information (i.e. the UE shall select first the highest priority PLMN in the Non-3GPP access node selection information that is contained in the candidate list of PLMNs). If the Non-3GPP access node selection information does not contain any of the PLMNs in the candidate list of PLMNs, or the UE is not configured with the Non-3GPP access node selection information, or the UE was not able to connect to a non-3GPP access node in any of the PLMNs included in the Non-3GPP access node selection information and in the candidate list of PLMN, then the UE shall select a PLMN included in the candidate list of PLMNs based on its own implementation means."
Start State: Not explicitly defined
Condition: The UE is in a state where it needs to select a PLMN for non-3GPP access, triggered by one of the following: 1) UE is registered via 3GPP access to a PLMN not in the candidate list, 2) UE is not registered via 3GPP access to any PLMN, or 3) UE failed to connect to a non-3GPP access node as per a prior condition.
Action: The UE executes a two-step PLMN selection from the candidate list. First, it attempts to select a PLMN based on the prioritized list in the configured Non-3GPP access node selection information, choosing the highest priority PLMN that is also in the candidate list. If this is not possible (due to missing configuration, no overlap between lists, or failure to connect to any non-3GPP node in the overlapping PLMNs), the UE selects a PLMN from the candidate list using its own implementation.
End State: A PLMN is selected from the candidate list for the UE's non-3GPP access procedure
============================================================
Event ID: 8328 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)

Condition: The UE cannot select a suitable non-3GPP access node in any of the PLMNs from the candidate list.
Action: The UE shall stop the ongoing non-3GPP access node selection procedure.
End State: The non-3GPP access node selection procedure is terminated.

Event ID: 8331 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "2. When the selection is required for an IMS service, the UE shall choose a non-3GPP access node type (i.e. ePDG or N3IWF) based on the "Preference" parameter specified in clause 6.3.6.1, unless the UE has its 5GS capability disabled in which case it shall choose an ePDG independent of the "Preference" parameter setting."
Start State: Not explicitly defined
Condition: A selection for an IMS service is required.
Action: The UE chooses a non-3GPP access node type (ePDG or N3IWF). If the UE's 5GS capability is enabled, the choice is based on the "Preference" parameter. If the UE's 5GS capability is disabled, the UE chooses an ePDG regardless of the "Preference" parameter.

Event ID: 8332 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "If the "Preference" parameter for the selected PLMN indicates that ePDG is preferred, the UE shall attempt to select an ePDG. If the "Preference" parameter for the selected PLMN indicates that N3IWF is preferred, the UE shall attempt to select an N3IWF."
Start State: The UE has selected a PLMN for non-3GPP access and has a "Preference" parameter for that PLMN.
Condition: The UE needs to select a non-3GPP interworking function (ePDG or N3IWF) for the selected PLMN.
Action: The UE evaluates the "Preference" parameter. If it indicates ePDG is preferred, the UE attempts to select an ePDG. If it indicates N3IWF is preferred, the UE attempts to select an N3IWF.
End State: The UE has initiated the selection attempt for the preferred non-3GPP interworking function (ePDG or N3IWF).
============================================================
Event ID: 8333 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "If the selection fails, including the case when, during the registration performed over either 3GPP or non-3GPP access, the UE receives the IMS Voice over PS session Not Supported over Non-3GPP Access indication (specified in clause 5.16.3.2a), the UE shall attempt selecting the other non-3GPP access node type in the selected PLMN, if any. If that selection fails too, or it is not possible, then the UE shall select another PLMN, according to the procedure specified bullet 3c) above."
Start State: The UE is in a state where its initial selection of a non-3GPP access node type within a selected PLMN has failed, or it has received an "IMS Voice over PS session Not Supported over Non-3GPP Access" indication during registration.
Condition: The failure of the initial non-3GPP access node type selection, or the reception of the "IMS Voice over PS session Not Supported over Non-3GPP Access" indication during registration.
Action: The UE shall first attempt to select the other non-3GPP access node type within the same selected PLMN. If that second selection also fails or is not possible, the UE shall select another PLMN as per the procedure in bullet 3c).
End State: The UE has either successfully selected the other non-3GPP access node type in the same PLMN, or it has initiated the procedure to select another PLMN.
============================================================
Event ID: 8334 (Derived from 6.3.6.3)
Sentence: "3. When the selection is required for a non-IMS service, the UE shall perform the selection by giving preference to the N3IWF independent of the "Preference" parameter setting. If the N3IWF selection fails, or it is not possible, the UE should select another PLMN based on the procedure specified in clause 4.5.4.4 of TS 23.402 [43] and shall attempt to select an N3IWF in this PLMN. If the UE fails to select an N3IWF in any PLMN, the UE may attempt to select an ePDG according to the procedure specified in clause 4.5.4.5 of TS 23.402 [43]."
Start State: Not explicitly defined
Condition: Selection of a non-3GPP access network is required for a non-IMS service.
Action: The UE shall first attempt to select an N3IWF, giving it preference regardless of the "Preference" parameter. If this fails, the UE should select another PLMN per clause 4.5.4.4 of TS 23.402 [43] and attempt N3IWF selection in that PLMN. If N3IWF selection fails in any PLMN, the UE may attempt to select an ePDG per clause 4.5.4.5 of TS 23.402 [43].
End State: The UE has either selected an N3IWF, selected an ePDG, or failed to select a non-3GPP access network.
============================================================

Event ID: 8345 (Derived from 6.3.6.4.2)
Sentence: "If the UE is attached to 5GC via an N3IWF that is located in the same country as the country in which the UE is currently located and the AMF has previously indicated support for emergency services over non-3GPP access as defined in clause 5.16.4.1, the UE reuses the existing N3IWF connection for emergency services. Start State: The AMF needs to establish AM Policy Association and UE Policy Association for a UE.
Condition: The AMF is triggered to select a PCF instance for policy associations (e.g., during UE registration or a policy association establishment procedure).

In a non-roaming scenario, it selects a PCF instance for AM Policy Association and reuses the same instance for UE Policy Association. In a roaming scenario, it selects a V-PCF instance for AM Policy Association and reuses the same V-PCF instance for UE Policy Association.
End State: The AMF has selected and associated with the appropriate PCF (or V-PCF) instance for both AM Policy and UE Policy Associations.

The SMF may decide based on operator policy either to use the same PCF or select a new PCF. Condition: The AMF decides, based on operator policy, to potentially use the same PCF for both AMF and SMF policy control. A specific condition is met if the DNN and S-NSSAI of the PDU Session match an entry in the PCF Selection Assistance Information from the UDM.

Upon receiving this indication along with the PCF ID, the SMF is mandated to select the same PCF instance for SM Policy Control. Otherwise, the SMF may, based on operator policy, decide to use the forwarded PCF or select a new one.

Sentence: "PCF(s) that need to receive AF requests that targets a DNN (and slice) and/or a group of UEs subscribe to receive notifications from the UDR about such AF request information. The PCF(s) take(s) the received AF request information into account when making policy decisions for existing and future relevant PDU Sessions. In the case of existing PDU Sessions, the policy decision of the PCF instance(s) may trigger a PCC rule(s) change from the PCF to the SMF."

Action: The PCF(s) subscribe to receive notifications about the AF request information from the UDR(s). They then take this information into account for policy decisions for relevant PDU Sessions. For existing PDU Sessions, this may trigger a PCC rule change from the PCF to the SMF.

It is used by the SMF to select a CHF that manages the online charging or offline charging for a PDU Session of a subscriber. It is used by the AMF to select a CHF that manages the online charging or offline charging for 5G connection and mobility of a subscriber. It is used by the SMSF to select a CHF that manages the online charging or offline charging for the SMS over NAS transactions of a subscriber. It is used by the PCF to select a CHF that manages the spending limits for a subscriber and/or a PDU Session of a subscriber."
Start State: Not explicitly defined
Condition: A Network Function (SMF, AMF, SMSF, or PCF) requires a CHF to manage charging or spending limits for a subscriber's session, connection, mobility, or SMS transactions.
Action: The respective Network Function (SMF, AMF, SMSF, or PCF) invokes the CHF discovery and selection function to select an appropriate CHF based on the required charging context (PDU Session, 5G connection/mobility, SMS over NAS, or spending limits).
End State: A suitable CHF is selected for the specific charging or spending limit management task.

Event ID: 8509 (Derived from 6.3.12.1)
Sentence: "When the UE wants to discover the PLMN List(s) supported by a non-3GPP access network and the non-3GPP access network supports ANQP, the UE shall send an ANQP query to the non-3GPP access network requesting "3GPP Cellular Network" information. If the non-3GPP access network supports interworking with one or more PLMNs, the response received by the UE includes a "3GPP Cellular Network" information element containing one or more of the above three PLMN Lists. The PLMN List-1 and the PLMN List-2 are specified in TS 23.402 [43] and indicate support of interworking with EPC in one or more PLMNs. The PLMN List-3 is a list used to indicate support of interworking with 5GC in one or more PLMNs. When the non-3GPP access network does not support ANQP, how the UE discovers the PLMN List(s) supported by the non-3GPP access network is not defined in the present specification."
Start State: The UE is connected to or scanning a non-3GPP access network and has a need to discover the supported PLMN list(s). The UE is aware of the ANQP capability of the access network.
Condition: The UE wants to discover the PLMN List(s) supported by a non-3GPP access network, and the non-3GPP access network supports ANQP.
Action: The UE sends an ANQP query to the non-3GPP access network requesting "3GPP Cellular Network" information. If the access network supports interworking, it responds with a "3GPP Cellular Network" information element containing one or more PLMN Lists (List-1, List-2 for EPC interworking, or List-3 for 5GC interworking).
End State: The UE has received a response containing the supported PLMN list(s) if the access network supports ANQP and interworking. If the access network does not support ANQP, the discovery method is undefined.

Action: If the connected PLMN is in the list of available PLMNs, the UE selects it. If it is not in that list but is present in the "Non-3GPP access node selection information", the UE selects it and executes the combined ePDG/N3IWF selection procedure per clause 6.3.6.3.
End State: The UE has selected the PLMN it is connected to via 3GPP access, potentially initiating a combined ePDG/N3IWF selection procedure.

Sentence: "- The N5CW device selects the HPLMN if the N5CW device has a USIM or is pre-configured with an HPLMN, if the HPLMN is included in the list of available PLMNs. Otherwise, the N5CW device selects an E-HPLMN (Equivalent HPLMN), if an E-HPLMN is included in the list of available PLMNs. If the list of available PLMNs does not include the HPLMN and does not include an E- HPLMN, the N5CW device stops the access network selection procedure."
Start State: The N5CW device is performing an access network selection procedure and has a list of available PLMNs.
Condition: The N5CW device has a USIM or is pre-configured with an HPLMN.
Action: The N5CW device evaluates the list of available PLMNs. If the HPLMN is in the list, it selects the HPLMN. If not, it checks for an E-HPLMN in the list. If an E-HPLMN is present, it selects an E-HPLMN. If neither the HPLMN nor an E-HPLMN is in the list, it stops the access network selection procedure.
End State: The N5CW device has either selected an HPLMN, selected an E-HPLMN, or stopped the access network selection procedure.

Sentence: "The NF consumers (e.g. NWDAF) may utilize the NRF to discover AF instance(s) in the MNO domain, i.e. trusted AF(s), unless AF information is available by other means, e.g. locally configured in NF consumers. The NRF provides NF profile(s) of AF instance(s) to the NF consumers."

Event ID: 8727 (Derived from 6.3.25)
Sentence: "The NF consumer (e.g. NWDAF) may select an AF instance, in the MNO domain, considering one or multiple combination(s) of the S-NSSAI and DNN corresponding to an AF and the EventID(s) supported by an AF to provide the input data required for generation of analytics. Action: The NF consumer selects an AF instance in the MNO domain. The selection may be based on: 1) combinations of S-NSSAI and DNN corresponding to the AF and the EventID(s) it supports; 2) the Application Id(s) supported by the AF, if data is needed only for specific applications; 3) the Internal-Group Identifier supported by the AF, if data is needed for a specific UE group.
End State: An appropriate AF instance is selected for providing the required input data.

Event ID: 8730 (Derived from 6.3.26)
Sentence: "- NF consumers or SCP may have all the NRF services instances and their endpoint addresses locally configured."
Start State: NF consumers or SCP rely on dynamic discovery via the NRF or other means to obtain service instance information.
Condition: A deployment choice or configuration decision is made to use local configuration instead of, or as a fallback to, dynamic discovery.
Action: An operator or system configures the NF consumer or SCP with a complete local list of NRF service instances and their corresponding endpoint addresses.
End State: The NF consumer or SCP possesses a locally stored, pre-configured set of NRF service instances and endpoints, enabling direct access without querying the NRF for discovery.
============================================================
Event ID: 8731 (Derived from 6.3.26)
Sentence: "- NF consumers or SCP may have the endpoint address of a NRF discovery service locally configured and utilize it to discover the NRF(s) and get the NF profile(s) of the NRF(s)."
Start State: NF consumers or SCP do not have access to the NRF(s) or their NF profiles.
Condition: NF consumers or SCP have a locally configured endpoint address for an NRF discovery service.
Action: NF consumers or SCP utilize the locally configured endpoint address to discover the NRF(s) and retrieve the NF profile(s) of the NRF(s).
End State: NF consumers or SCP have discovered the NRF(s) and obtained their NF profile(s).
============================================================
Event ID: 8732 (Derived from 6.3.26)
Sentence: "- NF consumers (e.g. v-NRF) or SCP may have endpoint addresses of the NRF bootstrapping service and utilize it to discover the NRF service instances and their endpoint addresses. The NRF bootstrapping service is a version independent API, which may be especially useful over roaming interfaces."
Start State: NF consumers or SCP do not have endpoint addresses for the target NRF service instances.
Condition: NF consumers (e.g., v-NRF) or SCP possess the endpoint address of the NRF bootstrapping service.
Action: NF consumers or SCP utilize the NRF bootstrapping service (a version-independent API) to discover the NRF service instances and their endpoint addresses.
End State: NF consumers or SCP have discovered the endpoint addresses of the target NRF service instances.

Event ID: 8733 (Derived from 6.3.26)
Sentence: "- The NF consumer, e.g. AMF, may use the Nnssf_NSSelection service to get the endpoint address of a NRF discovery service for a certain slice."
Start State: The NF consumer (e.g., AMF) requires the endpoint address of an NRF discovery service for a specific network slice.
Condition: The NF consumer needs to discover an NRF instance that serves a particular slice.
Action: The NF consumer invokes the Nnssf_NSSelection service to obtain the requested NRF endpoint address.
End State: The NF consumer has obtained the endpoint address of the NRF discovery service for the specified slice.
